# STATE

> **Unchecked prose.** This file is written by hand; no script checks what its sentences claim. What is machine-checked: the Lean statements in `Checked/`, their docstrings as far as `scripts/trust/check_prose.py` reads them, `TRUST_MAP.md` (generated), `CLAIMS.md` (every headline's claim generated from its Lean statement: rely on it, not on docstrings) and the certified numbers of `FINDINGS.md` (tagged ✓). When this file and those disagree, those win.

## Mathematics/physics: Thomas–Fermi initial slope (THE RESULT parts 2 and 1; run 2026-11-06T1828Z): PROVED in Work + AUDITED
- **PROVED in Work** (`Work/Math/ThomasFermi.lean`, `tf_initial_slope`; frozen 67f4410, statement and module doc unchanged; proof 7c25ba6; local build, axioms standard): every y with y' = φ on [0, ∞) (right derivative at 0), φ continuous at 0 from the right, φ' = y^{3/2}/√x on (0, ∞), y(0) = 1, y > 0 on [0, ∞), y → 0 at ∞ has −1.58807103 ≤ φ(0) ≤ −1.58807102.
- **AUDITED:** blind restatement (Sonnet; deriv (deriv y), no continuity hypothesis on y' at 0) closed by `Work.Math.ThomasFermiBlindClosed.blind_closed` (continuity derived, `deriv_near`); referee `runs/2026-11-06T1828Z/referee_tf.md` **STANDS** (own B = −1.58807102261137531271868450946…, margins 7.39·10⁻⁹ / 2.61·10⁻⁹; any two functions meeting the hypotheses agree; existence classical).
- Kind (part 2): digits in print are floating point (Amore–Boyd–Fernández 2012, 28 digits "believed"; OEIS A231786). Prior art still to read: Iacono 2008, J. Phys. A 41 455204 (analytic a-priori bounds; record only).

- **Route** (`Checked/Math/BEHCell.lean`, `BEHData.lean`, `BEHChain.lean`, `BEHTail.lean`, `BEHMain.lean`): the old cells in (e^w, φ) have an absolute Lipschitz constant ≥ 1, so their enclosure grows like e^s; new cells in homology variables u = s e^w/(−φ), v = −sφ (field (u(3 − u − v)/s, v(u − 1)/s), Lipschitz box around the Taylor polynomials) grow like s^2.5. 156 kernel-checked cells on [7, 150] from the state at radius 7 of `Checked/Math/BEEnd.lean` (2u − v < 0 to 67.00752, bracket cell with (2u − v)' > 0, 2u − v > 0 to 150); u v³ = 4π m² monotone on either side of the zero ξ₂ of 2u − v; Volterra's function beyond 150 with the convex-duality bound from below (u v³ > 6.059).

- **Route:** run 1228Z's reversed-flow stack generic in K (`Checked/FSRev/`), four generated certificate modules (over, slow, tube, printed ball) and the identification lemma of `Checked/FSRev/RIdent.lean` (clamped = unclamped while strictly inside the clamps). **Non-vacuity of the overshoot statement (run 1828Z):** `Checked/FSRev/RGlobal.lean` continues the solution past the chain with a dissipative clamped field (f clamped to [0, 2t + 4]; energy Q(q) + W(p) with the clamps inside is nonincreasing whatever the clamps do; IVT keeps every clamp inactive), `Checked/FSRev/R1988PrintedEnd.lean` (end state from the last cell's ranges), `Checked/FSRev/RPrintedNV.lean` (glue + scale back).
- **Necessity:** hypotheses 2 (p' = q), 3 (equation for q), 6 (the printed wall value) needed (`Checked/Trust/NecessityG.lean`); 1, 4, 5 open (disclosed).
- **Audit:** run 1228Z (blind restatement closed in `Work/FalknerSkan/Neg1988RevBlindClosed.lean`; referee STANDS-WITH-CORRECTIONS, independent −0.005157420177929). Candidate C-FS-1988R (FINDINGS).
- **Not yet:** uniqueness of the reverse-flow branch; necessity of hypotheses 1, 4, 5 (f' = p, f(0) = 0, p(0) = 0: need solutions for other initial data that never overshoot).

## Fluid mechanics: the attached Falkner–Skan wall shear at β = −0.1988, and the attached-flow stack generic in K (THE RESULT parts 2 and 5; run 2026-11-11T1028Z): promoted to Checked + AUDITED; PROVED once a main run is green on every job
- **Headlines:** `Checked.FalknerSkan.NegWallShear.fs_neg_wall_shear_window` — every solution of f''' + f f'' + β(1 − f'²) = 0, β = −1988/10000, with f(0) = f'(0) = 0, f' → 1, f'' > 0 on [0, ∞) and, for every M, eventually M(1 − f') ≤ f'', has 0.0052181 < f''(0) < 0.0052182; `fs_neg_wall_shear_exists_decay` — such a solution exists (Work names `fs_neg1988_window`, `fs_neg1988_exists_decay`, frozen 50de3b1 by run 0828Z, statements unchanged). Axioms standard.

Certificates at K = 2500/497: `N1988Lo` (slow approach at A = 11703189/10⁹, 134 cells) and `N1988Hi` (overshoot at A = 58517/5000000, 142 cells), from run 0828Z's generator (`runs/2026-11-11T0828Z/scripts/make_neg1988.sh`) and the new emitter `runs/2026-11-11T1028Z/scripts/emit_att_wc.py`. Every module compiled first time; the port took ≈ 25 min.

Referee (Opus, `runs/2026-11-11T1028Z/referee/REPORT.md`): STANDS-WITH-CORRECTIONS (statements right; independent f''(0) = 0.00521818788(4) by collocation with the parabolic-cylinder tail; the wrong printed values are truncation errors at η∞ ≈ 7.5–7.8; one paper prints the correct 0.005218 — typeset-read this run, `runs/2026-11-11T1028Z/typeset/guo2020_p33_table8.png`). NV witness; necessity 1–8 of the window disclosed open (as the β = −1/10 sibling).

## Fluid mechanics: the Falkner–Skan separation value β* (THE RESULT parts 2 and 5; run 2026-11-11T0232Z): promoted to Checked + AUDITED; PROVED once a main run is green on every job (read next run)
- **Work theorems (2):** `Work/FalknerSkan/Separation.lean` — `fs_separation_exists` (frozen fbaa07e, proved 3e4f5c0): there is β with −0.1988377351 < β < −0.1988377350 and a solution of f''' + f f'' + β(1 − f'²) = 0 on [0, ∞) with f(0) = f'(0) = 0, **zero wall shear f''(0) = 0**, f' → 1, f'' > 0 on (0, ∞) and, for every M, eventually M(1 − f') ≤ f''; `Work/FalknerSkan/SeparationUnique.lean` — `fs_separation_unique` (frozen d6e6727, proved f6528b6): two such profiles (any β₁, β₂) have β₁ = β₂. Together: the separation value is unique and lies in the window. Axioms standard; no sorry.
- **Why the decay class:** for β* < β < 0 the zero-shear shot also reaches f' → 1, but with an algebraic tail (1 − f' ~ C η^{2β}); the condition "for every M eventually M(1 − f') ≤ f''" excludes exactly those (referee §1.1). Without it every β in the window would qualify.
- **Route (existence):** a *parameter of the equation* is shot, not a start value: scaled F''' + K F F'' − (1 − F'²) = 0, K = −1/β, fixed start (0, 0, 0) (`Work/FSSep/SBase.lean`: field `vS K`, events `Over`, `SlowS` with θ = 11/15 valid for K ≥ 5, no sign condition on q along the path since q(0) = 0). Certified Over at K = 10¹⁰/1988377351 (`SOverLo`, 128 cells, end margin 2.3·10⁻¹¹) and SlowS at K = 10¹⁰/1988377350 (`SSlowHi`, 132 cells, margins ≥ 5.4·10⁻¹¹), h = 1/32, degree 12, 10⁻¹⁸ coefficients, amplification e^3.6, with the K-parameterised kernel-evaluated cells `SCellWC` (`CellProp K`; generator `runs/2026-11-11T0232Z/scripts/gen_sep_wc.py`, `emit_sep_wc.py`, `port_scellwc.py`, notes `SEP_CELLS.md`); flow continuous in the coefficient K (flowK_cont in `Work/FSSep/SFlow.lean`, Grönwall for approximate trajectories); clamps inactive with q(0) = 0 (`SUnclamp`); supremum argument over K and scaling back (`Work/FSSep/SAssemble.lean`).
- **Route (uniqueness):** referee's §1.3 (β-form Crocco variables): z² comparison at the wall (w = z², w' → −2β as t → 0, so β₁ < β₂ gives z₁ > z₂ near t = 0), no crossing, the U′ tail estimate with the decay of the larger-β profile (`Work/FSSep/UCrocco.lean`, `UCompare.lean`, `UPair.lean`).
- **Audit:** blind restatements (Sonnet, words only) closed in Lean: `runs/2026-11-11T0232Z/blind/` (global C³ f with iterated two-sided derivatives; closed by the C³ extension −βη³/6 for η < 0) in `Work/FalknerSkan/SepBlindClosed.lean`; `blind2/` (one-sided derivatives within [0, ∞), C³ on [0, ∞)) in `SepUniqueBlindClosed.lean`. Referee (Opus, `runs/2026-11-11T0232Z/referee/REPORT.md`): **STANDS**; independent β* = −0.198837735046677546889047131815… (mpmath `odefun` + parabolic-cylinder tail matching, ≈ 35 digits; scipy 14 digits), inside the window with margins 5.3·10⁻¹¹ and 4.7·10⁻¹¹; its existence-only caveat is resolved by `fs_separation_unique`.
- **Kind (part 2):** the value is classical (Hartree 1937, −0.1988); best correct printed value found has 9 digits (Barlow et al., arXiv:1907.09912: −0.198837735); **no rigorous or computer-assisted enclosure found** (limited search). The referee found two printed values wrong in the 7th–8th digit (Fazio, arXiv:1212.5057 and 2011.07725; FINDINGS candidate C-FS-SEP, not yet read from the typeset text) and published tables of f''(0) at β = −0.1988 wrong in the 4th digit.
- **Promotion (same run, local Lean):** headlines `Checked.FalknerSkan.Separation.fs_separation_exists` and `Checked.FalknerSkan.SeparationEqual.fs_separation_equal` (the Work theorem `fs_separation_unique` under a name and module without the strength word, V8/V13); modules `Checked/FSSep/*` and `Checked/FalknerSkan/{Separation,SeparationEqual}.lean` by `runs/2026-11-11T0232Z/scripts/promote_sep.py`. Blind literal reading of the window ends and of the open half-line (`Checked/Trust/SecondReadingFSSepW.lean`; third reader — the first brief leaked the template denominator, the second brief's commit carried a module rename; all three readers gave the same constants). NV 155/155. Necessity: the two decay conditions of `fs_separation_equal` NEEDED (`Checked/Trust/NecessityC.lean`: the certified slow-approach solution at β = −0.1988377350, zero wall shear, algebraic tail, `Checked/FSSep/SSlowSol.lean`); the other 16 disclosed open in LEDGER. Local: full build, StatementDeps (relock), Claims, every Python trust check incl. `--base 3e54961` green.
- **Run 2026-11-11T0828Z, the printed value (on a branch, not merged):** `Checked/FalknerSkan/SeparationPrinted.lean` (branch `wip/sep-printed-promo`, efdb2d6): the β of every zero-wall-shear solution in the decay class lies below −0.1988376, the value Fazio prints (arXiv:1212.5057v1 Fig. 4, arXiv:2011.07725v1 Fig. 7; typeset crops `runs/2026-11-11T0828Z/typeset/`; blind literal reading `fsSepPrintedFazio`, brief 309689c). Audit: blind restatement (Sonnet, words only, `runs/2026-11-11T0828Z/blind/`) closed from the theorem (`Work/FalknerSkan/SepPrintedBlindClosed.lean` on the branch, work job green); NV witness; necessity 1, 2, 3 NEEDED (`Checked/Trust/NecessityE.lean`), 4–9 open; nanoda 4/4 green on the new modules (each needs a certified solution at the printed β outside one condition). Referee: run 0232Z's independent β*.
- **Not yet:** the 16 open hypotheses of `fs_separation_equal`; the endpoint events in β-form for unclamped solutions; merging the printed-value promotion (exact next step 1).

## Fluid mechanics: Falkner–Skan with adverse pressure gradient, β < 0 (THE RESULT parts 2 and 5; runs 2026-11-09T0828Z, 1028Z): promoted to Checked + AUDITED, PROVED (CI 42635575605 of 3dcc21f, a descendant, green on every job incl. the checked job; read run 2026-11-09T1428Z)

Blind literal readings: `Checked/Trust/SecondReadingFSNeg.lean` (m = −1/21 → β = −1/10, edge value, decay condition with the reader's own edge asymptotics; brief 600017e, reading 0c8270b), `Checked/Trust/SecondReadingFSWall.lean` (wall value q 0; eef88ef → 8eb3ece). Prior art (`runs/2026-11-09T1028Z/screen/REPORT.md`): substance classical for β* < β < 0 (Iglisch & Kemnitz 1955, Hartman 1964, via Veldman & van de Vooren 1980); largest-wall-shear form and any machine-checked version not found.
- **Work theorems (4):** `Work/FalknerSkan/NegDecay.lean` — `fs_neg_decay_max_shear` (β < 0: if one solution of the boundary-value problem has q > 0 on [0, ∞) and, for every M, eventually M(1 − p) ≤ q, every other solution with q > 0 has wall shear q₂(0) ≤ q(0)) and `fs_neg_decay_equal` (two solutions in that class agree), frozen 881ec81; `Work/FalknerSkan/NegTenth.lean` — `fs_neg_tenth_exists_decay` (β = −1/10: a solution in that class exists) and `Work.FalknerSkan.NegTenth.fs_neg_tenth_not_unique` (Work helper; β = −1/10: two solutions with q > 0, 0 ≤ p ≤ 1, p → 1 and eventually q < 3(1 − p), with q(0) = 1/(2√10) and 3/(4√10): uniqueness in the textbook class 0 ≤ f' ≤ 1 is false, so the decay condition of `fs_neg_decay_equal` cannot be dropped), frozen c95ce4c. Axioms standard; no sorry.
- **Route:** scaling F(s) = c f(s/c), c = √(−β), to F''' + K F F'' + 1 − F'² = 0, K = 1/(−β) (`Work/FalknerSkan/NegCore.lean`); Crocco map with the adverse sign (`NegCrocco`), no-crossing lemma (`NegNoCross`: the cooperative comparison of the β > 0 rows is false here; the pressure term cancels at a crossing instead), Theorem U′ (`NegUPrime`). β = −1/10: `Work/FSNeg/` — clamped field and events Over/Slow (`NBase`), kernel-evaluated weighted cells with the sign-flipped one-sided bound (`NCellWC`; chains `NSlowHalf`, `NSlow34`, `NOver11`, h = 1/16, 18–20 cells, margins ≥ 0.02; generator `runs/2026-11-09T0828Z/scripts/gen_neg_wc.py`, `emit_neg_wc.py`), forward-invariant Slow region (`NSlowInv`), limit (`NLimit`), Riccati upgrade (`NRiccati`), clamps never active (`NUnclamp`, energy q²/2 + p³/3 − p), assembly by supremum on [3/4, 11/10] (`NAssemble`).
- **Audit:** referee (Opus, `runs/2026-11-09T0828Z/referee/REPORT.md`): every lemma of the research note confirmed, six fixable gaps (all closed in the Lean), two false float remarks; numbers reproduced independently at 50/65 digits. Blind restatements (Sonnet, words only): `runs/2026-11-09T0828Z/blind/` closed in `Work/FalknerSkan/NDBlindClosed.lean`, `runs/2026-11-09T0828Z/blind2/` closed in `Work/FalknerSkan/NTBlindClosed.lean`. Prior-art screen (`runs/2026-11-09T0828Z/screen/REPORT.md`; primary sources Coppel 1960, Hartman Ch. XIV, Hastings 1972 not read): decay-class selection classical (Hartree, Stewartson; the rigorous form is to be checked against Coppel/Hartman), algebraic-decay continua known non-rigorously (Brown–Stewartson 1965, Brauner et al. 1982), no computer-assisted or formal treatment of Falkner–Skan found.
- **Kind (part 2):** uniqueness theorem likely classical (unverified); non-uniqueness at a fixed β with explicit wall shears, and existence by certified shooting: new in rigorous form as far as the screen found.
- **Not yet:** the ten-decimal window and the reversed-flow branch: proved in Work by run 1228Z (section above).

## Fluid mechanics: the Falkner–Skan and stagnation-point boundary-value problems with no bound on f' (THE RESULT parts 2 and 5; run 2026-11-09T0028Z): eight headlines **PROVED** (CI 42552130020 of 4ca5437, a descendant of the promotion 3684e58, green on every job incl. the checked job; read run 2026-11-09T0228Z) + AUDITED
- **Question.** Do the window and uniqueness theorems of the class −1 ≤ f' ≤ 1 hold for every solution of f''' + f f'' + β(1 − f'²) = 0, f(0) = f'(0) = 0, f' → 1 (and the Homann form)? **Yes for 0 < β ≤ 1:** every solution has f'' > 0 (general-β helper theorem in `Checked/FalknerSkan/Limit.lean`, not a headline; core in `Checked/FalknerSkan/LimitCore.lean`, K = 1/β ≥ 1).
- **Headlines** (`Checked/FalknerSkan/Limit.lean`, promotion 3684e58; frozen d71bf54, Work proof 5ae2ab6, statements byte-identical): `hiemenz_limit_window`, `hiemenz_limit_equal`, `homann_limit_window`, `homann_limit_equal`, `fs_half_limit_window`, `fs_half_limit_equal`, `fs_fifth_limit_window`, `fs_fifth_limit_equal` — the `*_bounded_*` statements with the class hypothesis deleted.
- **Proof** (`runs/2026-11-09T0028Z/coppel/REPORT.md`, research subagent; re-derived in the main session): r = q'; (Λ r)' = (2 − K) Λ p q, Φ = Λ(f r − p q) with Φ' = −Λ q (q + 2(K − 1) f p), Ω = M(p² − 1 − 2 f q) with Ω' = 2(K − 2) M f² q (Λ = e^{K∫f}, M = e^{2∫f}); a solution with some q ≤ 0 has a last zero η₂ of q with p(η₂) < −1 (touch: q = 0, p = ±1 impossible by backward uniqueness); for 1 ≤ K ≤ 2 the descent gives f(η₂) < 0 and the ascent then forces p past 1; for K ≥ 2, Ω ≥ 0 after η₂ contradicts Ω < 0 far out. K ≥ 1 enters only in the sign of Φ'; for K = 0.5 a non-monotone solution exists numerically.
- **Audit:** blind restatement (Sonnet) closed for all nine (`Work/FalknerSkan/UBlindClosed.lean`); referee STANDS-WITH-CORRECTIONS (prose only, `runs/2026-11-09T0028Z/referee_unrestricted.md`). Literal readings: the existing blind readings of the bounded headlines (equation, boundary conditions, window) cover the new statements.
- **Kind (part 2):** classical — uniqueness without restriction on f' for 0 ≤ β ≤ 1 (Coppel 1960; Craven & Peletier 1972; per Fazio arXiv:2011.07725 read verbatim; primaries unread); the argument's origin is unknown. New: machine-checked, with the certified ten-decimal windows.
- **Not yet:** the general-β monotonicity theorem of `Limit.lean` as a headline (needs a blind reading of `β ≤ 1` and the β-form right-hand side); β = 1/10 (Work only); `scripts/trust/audits.txt` regeneration.

## Fluid mechanics: Hiemenz and Homann in the class −1 ≤ f' ≤ 1 — window, uniqueness, existence (THE RESULT parts 2 and 5; run 2026-11-08T1228Z): six headlines promoted to Checked + AUDITED, PROVED (CI 42635575605 of 3dcc21f, a descendant, green on every job incl. the checked job; read run 2026-11-09T1428Z)
- **Question.** Run 1028Z's theorems are about solutions with f'' > 0, a condition on the unknown's second derivative that a reader cannot check on a solution they do not have, and "same wall shear" is not "same solution". Does the bound −1 ≤ f' ≤ 1 on the velocity profile (which contains the class 0 < f' < 1 of the classical uniqueness theory) suffice, and are two such solutions equal?
- **Frozen** 804a187 (`Work/FalknerSkan/Bounded.lean`), **proved in Work** 9920ce2 (statements byte-identical; axioms standard), **promoted** to `Checked/FalknerSkan/Bounded.lean` with the tools `Checked/FalknerSkan/BoundedTools.lean`: `hiemenz_bounded_window`, `homann_bounded_window` (every solution with f(0) = f'(0) = 0, f' → 1 and −1 ≤ f' ≤ 1 on [0, ∞) has f''(0) strictly inside the Hiemenz, resp. Homann window); `hiemenz_bounded_equal`, `homann_bounded_equal` (two such solutions have the same f, f', f'' on [0, ∞)); `hiemenz_exists_bounded`, `homann_exists_bounded` (a solution with 0 ≤ f' < 1 and wall shear in the window).
- **Route (no new numerical certificate, ≈ 200 lines):** at a zero of f'' the equation gives f''' = −1 + f'² < 0 when |f'| < 1 (for every coefficient k of f f''), so f'' only crosses zero downwards; once f'' ≤ 0, f' stops increasing and cannot tend to 1 → f'' > 0 (fencing theorem). A touch f' = ±1 at η₁ > 0 is an interior extremum, so f''(η₁) = 0, and backward ODE uniqueness against the straight line f' ≡ ±1 (a solution for every k) forces f'(0) = ±1 ≠ 0. Then the Checked monotone window and Crocco comparison give the window and one wall shear, and forward uniqueness from (f, f', f'')(0) gives one solution.
- **Audit:** referee on the scratch statements STANDS-WITH-CORRECTIONS (`runs/2026-11-08T1228Z/referee_bounded.md`; corrections to run-record wording only); blind restatement (Sonnet, words only; C³ on [0, ∞) with iterated one-sided derivatives) closed in full, all six including existence (`Work/FalknerSkan/BBlindClosed.lean`); blind literal reading of the two bounds (`Checked/Trust/SecondReadingBound.lean`: route brief 817c726 → reading → copy).
- **Audit finding (run 1028Z's referee, §1):** "given f' → 1, f'' > 0 ⟺ f' < 1" — the step "q' < 0 at every later zero of q" needs f'² < 1; at a zero of f'' with f' ≤ −1, f''' ≥ 0. Whether f' < 1 alone suffices is **not proved** (≈ 6 200 shooting values per k: no counterexample; likely implied by the reported unrestricted uniqueness for β < 1, second-hand); no statement uses it.
- **Kind (part 2):** uniqueness of these flows is in print (second-hand here: Coppel 1960, Craven–Peletier 1972; for β = 1 sources disagree on the endpoint); new is the machine-checked statement for the class −1 ≤ f' ≤ 1, its window to ten decimals and "the solution" rather than "a solution". Not claimed beyond the literature.

## Fluid mechanics: the monotone solution of the Hiemenz and Homann problems (THE RESULT parts 2 and 5; run 2026-11-08T1028Z): four headlines PROVED (CI 42483811134 green on every job, read run 1228Z) + AUDITED; two in Work
- **Question.** The certified windows of `hiemenz_exists` / `homann_exists` held *a* boundary-value solution; is it the physical one (f'' > 0, monotone velocity profile), and can two physical solutions differ?
- **Frozen** 46a6de2 (`Work/FalknerSkan/MonotoneWindow.lean`) and 6d095c2 (`Work/FalknerSkan/MonotoneExists.lean`), **proved in Work** fe97390, 7e861d8 (statements byte-identical; axioms standard): `hiemenz_monotone_window`, `homann_monotone_window` (every solution with f(0) = f'(0) = 0, f' → 1 and f'' > 0 on [0, ∞) has f''(0) strictly inside (1.2325876568, 1.2325876569), resp. (1.3119376938, 1.3119376939)); `hiemenz_exists_monotone`, `homann_exists_monotone` (such a solution exists); `hiemenz_monotone_same_wall_shear`, `homann_monotone_same_wall_shear` (two such solutions have the same f''(0)).
- **Route (no new numerical certificate):** Crocco variables t = f', z = f'', F = f: dF/dt = t/z, dz/dt = −kF − (1 − t²)/z; for two solutions the differences form a cooperative linear system in (D, −E), so z₂ − z₁ never drops below its value at t = 0 while t < 1 (k ≥ 0) (`Checked/FalknerSkan/CroccoMap.lean`, `Checked/FalknerSkan/CroccoCompare.lean`). Comparing with the certified overshoot / undershoot IVP solutions at the window's ends (`Checked/Hiemenz/HIVP.lean`, `Checked/Homann/HmIVP.lean`) gives the window (`Checked/FalknerSkan/CroccoWindow.lean`), comparing two monotone solutions gives one wall shear; existence of a monotone solution: the shooting construction already proved q > 0 inside `no_event` (exposed by `runs/2026-11-08T1028Z/scripts/mk_mono.py`). Rejected route (measured): ball covers of the shooting parameter need ≈ 200–400 certified chains per side.
- **Audit:** blind restatement 1 (Sonnet) closed (`Work/FalknerSkan/MWBlindClosed.lean`); blind restatement 2 (`runs/2026-11-08T1028Z/blind2/`): same-wall-shear statements closed (`MEBlindClosed.lean`), its existence statements ask for an analytic solution (`ContDiffOn ℝ ⊤`) and are **not closed** (Underspecified: the headlines assert the first-order system's derivatives only); referee STANDS-WITH-CORRECTIONS (`referee_mw.md`: doc corrected; "f' < 1 ⟺ f'' > 0" claimed — run 1228Z's audit found the gap at f' ≤ −1).
- **Kind (part 2):** uniqueness of the Falkner–Skan solution in Coppel's class is in print (second-hand only here); new is the machine-checked statement that the *physical* solution's wall shear is in the ten-digit window.
- **Promoted** to `Checked/FalknerSkan/MonotoneWindow.lean`, `Checked/FalknerSkan/MonotoneExists.lean` (`runs/2026-11-08T1028Z/scripts/promote_mw.py`; NV witnesses from the monotone solutions). **Not yet:** the two same-wall-shear headlines (need a blind reading of the `q 0 = q₂ 0` literals and naming words); the class "f'' > 0" is a condition on the unknown's second derivative — superseded by run 1228Z's bounded-class headlines (section above), which include "same solution".

## Fluid mechanics: Falkner–Skan wall-shear table, rows β = 1/2 and β = 0 (THE RESULT parts 2 and 1; run 2026-11-08T0828Z): promoted to Checked + AUDITED, PROVED (CI 42635575605 of 3dcc21f, a descendant, green on every job incl. the checked job; read run 2026-11-09T1428Z)
- **Question.** Falkner–Skan g''' + g g'' + β(1 − g'²) = 0, g(0) = g'(0) = 0, g' → 1: the classical wall-shear table g''(0)(β) (Hartree 1937; modern tables print 6–15 digits). The similarity scaling F(s) = c f(s/c) maps f''' + a f f'' + b(1 − f'²) = 0 to coefficients a/c², b/c² and divides the wall shear by c; c = √2 maps Homann to β = 1/2 and the β = 0 problem to Blasius' f''' + f f''/2 = 0; Hiemenz is β = 1 as it stands.

Axioms propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound.
- **Audit:** blind restatement (Sonnet; iterated `derivWithin` on Ici 0, β a parameter) closed (`Work/FalknerSkan/FSBlindClosed.lean`); referee **STANDS** (`runs/2026-11-08T0828Z/referee_fs.md`: scaling re-derived with sympy, windows re-checked in exact rationals, own values 0.92768003983665113921…, 0.46959998836101330450…; prior art arXiv:1907.09912 prints 0.927680039836653 and 0.469599988361); blind literal reader (brief 1fad98b → reading fdf09cd → copy `Checked/Trust/SecondReadingFalknerSkan.lean`) derived the equation and β = 2m/(m + 1) from the wedge similarity variables and computed both wall values.
- **Kind (part 2):** certified forms of published numbers (both printed to more digits than certified); new is the machine-checked enclosure, for β = 0 for *every* solution, and the route: certified constants carried along a proved symmetry to new table rows with no new certificate.
- **Promoted** (this run; `runs/2026-11-08T0828Z/scripts/promote_fs.py`): `Checked/FalknerSkan/FSScale.lean`, `FalknerSkan.lean`; NV witness of `fs_zero_wall_shear` from the rescaled Blasius solution.
- **Not yet:** uniqueness for β = 1/2 (in print second-hand: Coppel 1960, Craven–Peletier 1972) — would turn "a solution" into "the solution" for Hiemenz and Homann at once; other rows need a β-parameterised generator (Hiemenz cells with β as a coefficient).

## Fluid mechanics: Homann axisymmetric stagnation-point flow (THE RESULT parts 2 and 5; run 2026-11-08T0428Z): PROVED (promotion 129f217, CI 42453268112 green on every job, read run 0628Z) + AUDITED
- **Question.** Axisymmetric stagnation-point flow (Homann 1936): f''' + 2 f f'' + 1 − f'² = 0 on [0, ∞), f(0) = f'(0) = 0, f'(η) → 1; wall shear f''(0). A genuine shooting constant (no scaling symmetry). Printed values are floating point (textbooks ≈ 1.312); the referee found no value with ≥ 10 digits in print and no published uniqueness or existence proof for this equation. **Corrected by run 2026-11-08T0828Z (LEDGER):** the equation is Falkner–Skan β = 1/2 under F(s) = √2 f(s/√2), whose wall shear 0.927680039836653 (= f''(0)/√2) is printed to 15 digits (arXiv:1907.09912), and uniqueness for 0 ≤ β ≤ 1 is reported in the literature (Coppel 1960; Craven–Peletier 1972; read second-hand).

- **Route:** the Hiemenz tools with the coefficient 2 (`runs/2026-11-08T0428Z/scripts/gen_homann_w.py`: Taylor recursion, third defect with 2·pg·pq, one-sided matrix at 2·mlo and the doubled q-corners), 96 + 96 kernel-evaluated cells (h = 1/16, degree 10, 18 digits, final radius 2.3·10⁻¹³; h = 1/8 failed: the crude range bound of p overshoots 1 near η ≈ 3.3). Existence modules ported by `scripts/port_homann.py` (text substitution; Lipschitz constant 2T + 12, field bound 4T + 13); every ported module compiled after one fix (the one-sided lemma's call site in the clamped cell module).
- **Audit:** blind restatement (Sonnet, `runs/2026-11-08T0428Z/blind/`) closed for all three statements (`Work/Homann/HomannBlindClosed.lean`); referee **STANDS** (`runs/2026-11-08T0428Z/referee_homann.md`: own f''(0) = 1.3119376938798051354816461706834 by event bisection and far-field matching with the closed-form decaying mode; event times 5.084 and 4.832); blind literal reader (brief 488aa29 → reading 1c1f4b0 → copy `Checked/Trust/SecondReadingHomann.lean`): derived the equation from the axisymmetric similarity variables, σ to 30 digits at two precisions (same method), H = 6 from its own event radii.

Uniqueness not formalised (and, per the referee, not found in the literature for this equation), so the theorem places *a* solution in the window.

## Fluid mechanics: Hiemenz stagnation-point flow (Falkner–Skan β = 1) (THE RESULT parts 2 and 5; runs 2026-11-07T2028Z, 2228Z): PROVED (CI 42427258702 green on every job) + AUDITED
- **Question.** Plane stagnation-point flow: f''' + f f'' + 1 − f'² = 0 on [0, ∞), f(0) = f'(0) = 0, f'(η) → 1; wall shear f''(0). - **Route:** weighted one-sided cells for the Hiemenz field (`Work/Hiemenz/HCellW.lean`: the Jacobian entry 2p makes the one-sided bound a four-corner Sylvester check; whole-cell enclosures carry the sign of p − 1 or q along the chain), 60 + 60 kernel-evaluated cells (h = 1/8, degree 12, 20 digits, radius 7·10⁻¹⁴, amplification e^5.5; ≈ 70 s and 9 MB per data module). Existence: clamped field whose cells prove the clamps inactive (`HCellWC.lean`), Picard–Lindelöf flow continuous in the parameter (`HExFlow.lean`), open exclusive events, supremum of the undershooting parameters, no event ⇒ boundary-value solution (`HExLimit.lean`; tangency excluded by backward uniqueness), assembly `HExAssemble.lean`. Three forks with exact interfaces (flow, clamped cells, limit) in parallel, ≈ 5–10 min each.

- **Kind (part 2):** digits in print are floating point (≤ 8 significant); claim is "first machine-checked enclosure" (10 digits). Uniqueness of the BVP solution (Coppel 1960) not formalised, so the theorem places *a* solution in the window.

## Fluid mechanics: Blasius boundary layer, wall shear (THE RESULT parts 2 and 5; runs 2026-11-07T1228Z, 1428Z, 1628Z): promoted to Checked + AUDITED, PROVED (CI 42635575605 of 3dcc21f, a descendant, green on every job incl. the checked job; read run 2026-11-09T1428Z)
- **Question.** Laminar flat-plate boundary layer: f''' + f f''/2 = 0 on [0, ∞), f(0) = f'(0) = 0, f'(η) → 1; the wall shear σ = f''(0) gives the Blasius skin friction. Floating-point values to 17 digits (Boyd 1999); rigorous enclosure in print ≈ 5 digits (Costin–Tanveer, arXiv:1303.1416); no machine-checked result found (`runs/2026-11-07T1228Z/prior_art_blasius.md`). Kind: not in print as a rigorous value beyond ≈ 5 digits.
- **Proved in Work** (frozen 4db71de; proofs f892958 and 56ce5fc; statements byte-identical; local builds, axioms propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound): `Work.Blasius.blasius_wall_shear` — every f with f' = p, p' = q, q' = −f q/2 on [0, ∞) (derivatives within [0, ∞)), f(0) = p(0) = 0, p → 1 has 0.33205733621519 ≤ q(0) ≤ 0.33205733621520; `Work.Blasius.blasius_exists` — such f, p, q exist. Only Mathlib definitions.
- **Route:** q never vanishes (Mathlib ODE uniqueness for the linear equation of q), so q(0) > 0; scale to g''(0) = 1 (`Work/Blasius/BTail.lean`); 288 kernel-evaluated validated cells of the 3-D system on [0, 9] (`BCell`, `BCellQ`, `BQData`: h = 1/32, degree 18, coefficients 10⁻³², radius 1.6·10⁻¹⁵; generator `runs/2026-11-07T1228Z/scripts/gen_blasius.py 9 1 32 18 32`, emitter `emit_blasius.py … 6 20`); tail past 9: p + (2/m) q non-increasing, so g'(∞) ∈ [g'(9), g'(9) + 2g''(9)/g(9)]; σ² g'(∞)³ = 1. Existence (`BExBase`, `BExFlow`, `BExBounds`, two forks): clamped global flow, clamps inactive by q t³ ≤ 12 ⇒ p ≤ 173/50, monotone limit, rescale.
- **Audit:** blind restatement (Sonnet; C³ on [0, ∞), `derivWithin`) closed by `Work.Blasius.BlindClosed.wall_shear_bounds` and `Work.Blasius.BlindClosed.exists_blasius_solution` (C³ by `contDiffOn_succ_iff_derivWithin`); referee STANDS (independent 50-digit σ = 0.33205733621519629894, margins 6.3·10⁻¹⁵ / 3.7·10⁻¹⁵), `runs/2026-11-07T1228Z/referee_blasius.md`.

## Mathematics: Townes soliton shooting bracket (THE RESULT parts 2 and 5; run 2026-11-07T0228Z): proved in Work + AUDITED (blind closure, referee)
- **Question.** The Townes soliton (positive radial ground state of ΔQ − Q + Q³ = 0 in the plane; the 2-D cubic NLS ground state) has central value θ(0) ≈ 2.20620086465 (referee's independent float: 2.20620086465074607); the screen of run 0028Z and this run's referee found no rigorous enclosure in print (referee READ list in `runs/2026-11-07T0228Z/referee_townes.md`; caveat: absence from searches is not absence).
- **Proved in Work** (frozen 0599a4c, proofs 2359ee4, statements unchanged): `Work.Townes.Shooting.townes_under` (θ(0) = 2.2062008646: φ > 0 at some r ≤ 68/5 with θ > 0 on [0, r]) and `Work.Townes.Shooting.townes_over` (θ(0) = 2.2062008647: θ < 0 at some r ≤ 68/5 with φ < 0 on (0, r]), for every S ≥ 68/5 and every solution on [0, S] (derivatives within [0, S], φ continuous at 0; no uniqueness assumed). Standard axioms only (local `#print axioms`).
- **Route:** singular start on [0, 3/10] by a degree-40 truncated series (`Work/Townes/TStart.lean`, template LEStart with u = r(φ − q)), then 2 × 123 validated Taylor cells of degree 12 in exact rationals (`TCell.lean`, generated `TCertsA1–5`, `TCertsB1–5`, chains `TChainA/B`; generator `runs/2026-11-07T0228Z/scripts/gen_townes.py`, emitter `emit_townes.py`); local build ≈ 40 min serial, 1.4 GB of oleans. Margins at r = 68/5 (generator output, unchecked prose): reference values φ ≈ 2·10⁻⁷ and θ ≈ −2.6·10⁻⁷ against a tube radius ≈ 8·10⁻⁸; the referee's float event radii are 13.521 and 13.499, so 68/5 is tight.
- **Audit:** blind restatement (Sonnet, informal claims only; two-sided derivatives on (0, 13.6), θ' continuous on [0, 13.6], θ'(0) = 0) closed by `Work.Townes.BlindClosed.blind_under` / `blind_over` (one-sided end derivatives from continuity of the derivative). Referee STANDS: hypotheses satisfiable and determine the solution; the two events exclude each other (proved by the referee in a scratch Lean file); swapped decimals make both false.
- **Existence (run 2026-11-07T0628Z): proved in Work + AUDITED** (frozen 78498eb, proof 14f7a24, statement byte-identical): `Work.Townes.Exists.townes_exists` — there is c ∈ (2.2062008646, 2.2062008647) and θ, φ on [0, ∞) with θ(0) = c, φ(0) = 0, φ continuous at 0, θ' = φ (one-sided at 0), φ' = −φ/r + θ − θ³ for r > 0, θ > 0, φ < 0 on (0, ∞), θ → 0. Only Mathlib definitions. Route: `TExLeft` (singular start on [0, 1/5]: fixed point of c + x²∫₀¹ (−u log u) g(f(xu)) du, Lipschitz in c by `ContractingWith.dist_fixedPoint_fixedPoint_of_dist_le'`), `TExFlow` (clamped flow from 1/5, Lipschitz in the initial state; clamped energy keeps the clamps inactive), `TExLimit` (no event at the critical value ⇒ positivity, sign, θ → 0; L = 1 excluded by rφ decreasing, 0 < L < 1 by φ' bounded below), `TExAssemble` (open exclusive events, sup argument; the shooting theorems enter as hypotheses of `core`). Local: every module green with `lake env lean`, axioms standard; `TownesExists` itself checked against stubs of the two shooting statements (the certificate chain is built by CI only: locally TCertsA1 took 17 min and a parallel module was OOM-killed). Audit: blind restatement (Sonnet; ContDiff ℝ 2 on all of ℝ, derivative-of-derivative form) closed in Lean from the frozen headline (`Work/Townes/TownesExistsBlindClosed.lean`, even extension, θ''(0) = (c − c³)/2 by l'Hôpital); referee STANDS (`runs/2026-11-07T0628Z/referee_exists.md`: mpmath 2.2062008646507460747, 5.07·10⁻¹¹ inside the lower end; no rigorous enclosure of the 2-D value in print to its search; R3: "the Townes value" needs Kwong 1989 uniqueness, not formalised).
- **Certificates rewritten (run 2026-11-07T0828Z, 08f4bd4; statements unchanged):** the shooting chains are now checked by kernel evaluation — `Work/Townes/TCellQ.lean` (cell, segment, chain and start checks: every side condition and both defect polynomials computed over ℚ by `Work/Sim/QPoly.lean`) with data `Work/Townes/TQData{A,B}.lean` (`runs/2026-11-07T0828Z/scripts/conv_townes.py`, same polynomials as run 0228Z); old modules in `_archive/Work/Townes/`. Local: both chains 2 min 20 s, 13 MB olean each, export of a chain +40 k lines, nanoda 95 s; CI work job of the branch (42360369090) green in 5 min 31 s.
- **Promotion (run 2026-11-07T0828Z, branch `wip/townes-checked` 21ba411 + 2e29c8f, not yet on main):** `runs/2026-11-07T0828Z/scripts/promote_townes.py` → `Checked/Sim/QPoly.lean`, `Checked/Townes/` (13 modules, specific imports; statements and proofs identical to Work up to the namespace); headlines townes_under, townes_over (module `Checked/Townes/TownesShooting.lean`) and townes_exists (`Checked/Townes/TownesExists.lean`) registered on the branch; NV witnesses from the IVP existence theorem of `Checked/Townes/TownesSolutions.lean`; **blind literal reading** `Checked/Trust/SecondReadingTownes.lean` (Sonnet reader, brief 9b0daf9 → reading ec5663a: own 45-digit shooting Q(0) = 2.20620086465074, L/U the ten-decimal roundings, event radii 13.521/13.499 → horizon 68/5; every statement literal covered under the strict rule); promotion audit `Work/Townes/TownesPromotionAudit.lean` (blind restatements closed from the Checked headlines). Relock from the author files of branch run 42360369090 (f3d1074; 183 headlines, NV 120/120; prose vocabulary `townes`, `undershoot`, `overshoot`, `existence`, `sided`), every trust script green locally; local nanoda on the trio's export green (45 552 declarations). **Branch CI run 42362919130 (8f34d65): checked job green on every blocking step** (Lean step 32 min: build, leanchecker, axiom audit; trust step 8 min: StatementDeps, NV, frozen registry, prose, literals, vocab; frozen step), work green, packet green, nanoda shards 1/2 passed (memoised from run 42360369090), shards 0/3 queued; main fast-forwarded to the branch at the end of the run.

## Mathematics: Gelfand critical parameter of the 3-D ball (THE RESULT part 5; runs 2026-11-06T1228Z, 1428Z): promoted to Checked (PROVED (CI 42635575605 of 3dcc21f, a descendant, green on every job incl. the checked job; read run 2026-11-09T1428Z)) + AUDITED
- **PROVED in Work** (`Work/Math/Gelfand.lean`, `gelfand_ball_critical`; frozen e0d7303, statement and module doc byte-identical; proof 848d907; local build, axioms standard): for every S ≥ 41/10 and every w on [0, S] differentiable within [0, S] with derivative φ, φ continuous at 0, φ' = −(2/s)φ − e^w on (0, S], w(0) = 0, the function s² e^{w(s)} attains its maximum over [0, S] at a point s₁ with 4.071496844 ≤ s₁ ≤ 4.071496845, and that maximum (the Gelfand critical parameter λ* of the radial branch in the unit ball) lies in [3.321992118, 3.321992119]. **Existence** (`Work/Math/GelfandExists.lean`, `exists_solution`, frozen e6b5543): such w, φ exist on [0, S] for every S ≥ 1/2.
- Kind (part 2 question): **not in print as a rigorous value** — referee (`runs/2026-11-06T1228Z/referee_gelfand.md`, STANDS): printed values are floating point, at most five digits (3.3220); no interval or computer-assisted enclosure found; the first-maximum-is-global argument is classical (Lyapunov function in Emden–Fowler variables) but was not machine-checked.
- Method: global maximum by the energy En = p²/2 + Λ − 2 log Λ (p = 2 + s w', Λ = s² e^w; En' = −p²/s), `Work/Math/GFinal.lean`; enclosure by the validated pipeline carried to e^w through the polynomial system E = e^w, φ = w' (`GCell.lean`), singular start with exp (`GStart.lean`, residual via |exp x − T₄(x)| ≤ |x|⁴·5/96, `GHelp.lean`); 92 cells generated by `runs/2026-11-06T1228Z/scripts/gen_g.py` → `emit_g.py` (`GCerts*.lean`, `GChain.lean`), final radius 3.1·10⁻¹². Margins (referee floats): 2.98·10⁻¹⁰ / 7.02·10⁻¹⁰ (s₁), 3.40·10⁻¹⁰ / 6.60·10⁻¹⁰ (λ*).
- Audit: blind restatement (Sonnet: maximum over every s > 0 as `IsGreatest`, deriv-based regular solution) closed in Lean (`Work/Math/GelfandBlindClosed.lean`; copy of the blind file `Work/Math/GelfandBlind.lean`); referee STANDS.
- **Promoted to Checked** (run 1428Z, commit fb999b2; `Checked/Math/Gelfand.lean`, `Checked/Math/GelfandExists.lean` and 11 supporting modules, statements byte-identical to the Work freezes up to the namespace): both registered as headlines (no program definitions in the statements), NV witnesses in `Checked/Trust/Witnesses.lean`, **blind literal reading of the radial problem** (Sonnet reader derived the coefficient 2 of w'/s, the exponent 2 of λ = s²e^w, the centre 0 and w(0) = 0 from the PDE and the boundary condition; `Checked/Trust/SecondReadingGelfand.lean`), the domain lengths 41/10 and 1/2 exempt by the new check_literals rule (a5). Local full build + relock + every check green; CI of fb999b2: read next run.
- **PDE pair promoted to Checked** (run 2026-11-07T0028Z, branch `wip/gpde-checked` fast-forwarded to main; copies by `runs/2026-11-07T0028Z/scripts/promote_gpde.py`, statements and proofs byte-identical to Work up to the namespace): `Checked/Math/{RadialLaplacian,GContFrom,GelfandODEBound,GelfandPDE,RadialC2,GProfile,GelfandPDEExists}.lean`; headlines `Checked.Math.GelfandPDE.gelfand_pde_radial_bound`, `Checked.Math.GelfandPDEExists.gelfand_pde_radial_exists`; NV witnesses (bound: from existence at λ = 3.321992118); blind literal reading `Checked/Trust/SecondReadingGelfandPDE.lean` (dimension 3, centre 0, radius 1, C², and the existence range's end 3.321992118 = the reader's own mpmath λ* = 3.32199211833982… rounded down); paraphrases rewritten for the closed vocabulary. Local full build, relock (180 headlines, NV 118/118), Claims and every Python check green; **PROVED (CI 42635575605 of 3dcc21f, a descendant, green on every job incl. the checked job; read run 2026-11-09T1428Z)** (read next run).
- **PDE side, nonexistence: PROVED in Work + AUDITED** (run 1628Z; `Work/Math/GelfandPDE.lean`, `gelfand_pde_radial_bound`, frozen 194b723, proof 1757139, statement and module doc byte-identical; local build, axioms standard): for every real λ and every u on ℝ³ (`EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 3)`) that is C² on the open unit ball, continuous on the closed ball, zero on the unit sphere, a function of ‖x‖ on the closed ball, with Δu + λeᵘ = 0 on the open ball (Δ = Mathlib's Laplacian): λ ≤ 3.321992119. Proof: radial Laplacian Δ(f∘‖·‖) = f'' + (n−1)/r·f' for x ≠ 0 in any finite-dimensional real inner product space (`Work/Math/RadialLaplacian.lean`); f(r) = u(r e₁) is C² on (−1, 1) and even, so f'(0) = 0; rescaling w(s) = f(s/k) − f(0), k = √λ·e^{f(0)/2}; continuation past any c < k from any start a > 0 (`Work/Math/GContFrom.lean`, the run-1228Z right piece generalised); `Checked.Math.Gelfand.gelfand_ball_critical` on the glued solution; λ = lim_{c→k⁻} c²e^{w(c)} (`Work/Math/GelfandODEBound.lean`). Blind restatement (Sonnet, from the words of the claim: radial as equal values on equal norms, conclusion False) closed (`Work/Math/GelfandPDEBlindClosed.lean`, copy `GelfandPDEBlind.lean`); referee STANDS (`runs/2026-11-06T1628Z/referee_gelfand_pde.md`: own 40-digit λ* = 3.3219921183398240, margin 6.6·10⁻¹⁰; not vacuous for λ > 0 by u = w(k‖x‖) − w(k); with 3.3219921 the statement is false; Δ is the usual sum of second partials; nit: the module doc's 'solutions with k < 41/10 that the ODE statement does not see' means solutions defined only up to k, which needed the continuation). Non-vacuity at λ = 0 (`nonvacuous_zero`) and **at λ = 3.321992118** (`Work.Math.GelfandPDEExistsBlindClosed.bound_sharp`: the bound cannot be lowered below 3.321992118).
- **PDE side, existence: PROVED in Work + AUDITED** (run 1628Z; `Work/Math/GelfandPDEExists.lean`, `gelfand_pde_radial_exists`, frozen 674cdc4, proof 03bbb04, statement and module doc identical; axioms standard): for every 0 < λ ≤ 3.321992118 there is a u with exactly the hypotheses of the bound (C² on the open ball, continuous on the closed ball, zero on the sphere, radial, Δu + λeᵘ = 0). Proof: (W, Φ) from `Checked.Math.GelfandExists.exists_solution`, IVT on s²e^{W(s)} below s₁ of the Checked theorem, u(x) = W(s‖x‖) + log(s²/λ); **C² at the origin** by `Work.Math.RadialC2.contDiffAt_zero_radial` (a radial function is C² at 0 if F1/ρ → L and F2 − F1/ρ → 0 at rate ρ and F − F(0) = O(ρ²); explicit derivatives, continuity of the second by opNorm bounds on vectors) with the centre bounds of `Work.Math.GProfile.profile_bounds` (−t/3 ≤ φ ≤ 0, −t²/6 ≤ w ≤ 0, |3t²φ + t³eʷ| ≤ t⁵/15, monotonicity only); Δu(0) = 3L (laplacian_radial_zero in `Work/Math/RadialC2.lean`). Blind restatement closed (`GelfandPDEExistsBlindClosed.blind_closed`); referee STANDS (`runs/2026-11-06T1628Z/referee_gelfand_pde_exists.md`: λ* = 3.32199211833982, margin 3.4·10⁻¹⁰; no junk satisfiability; the proof file was on disk, uncommitted, while the referee ran; it reports reading only the statement). **Together: the supremum of the λ with a radial classical solution lies in [3.321992118, 3.321992119].**
- Not yet: promotion of the PDE pair to Checked (modules import Mathlib wholesale; specific imports, registration, NV from `bound_sharp`, literal readings of 3.321992118/3.321992119 as certified targets); `scripts/trust/audits.txt` entries; Gidas–Ni–Nirenberg symmetry (all classical solutions radial); uniqueness of the maximiser; the next turning points (2.1085…, referee).
